278:             taskCount--;
279:         }
280: 
281:         H26_KeyProcess();
282: 
283:         if (Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_100ms_count) > 0U)
284:         {
285:             H26_Task100ms();
286:         }
287: 
288:         if (Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_200ms_count) > 0U)
289:         {
290:             H26_Task200ms();
291:         }
292:     }
293: 
294: #else
295: 
296:     Key_Init();
297:     IR8_Init();
298:     Motor_Init();
299:     Motor_StopAll();
300:     Encoder_Init();
301:     BeepLed_Init();
302:     DebugSerial_Init();
303:     CarBase_Init();
304: 
305: #if CAR_OLED_ENABLE
306:     OLED_Init();
307:     OLED_Clear();
308: #endif
309: 
310:     Timer_Init();
311: 
312:     DebugSerial_SendString("[boot,mode=safe-idle]\r\n");
313: 
314:     while (1)
315:     {
316:         uint8_t taskCount;
317: 
318:         (void)Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_1ms_count);
319: 
320:         taskCount = Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_5ms_count);
321:         if (taskCount > 0U)
322:         {
323:             App_Control_UpdateEncoderSpeed((uint16_t)taskCount * CAR_ENCODER_SPEED_PERIOD_MS);
324:         }
325: 
326:         taskCount = Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_10ms_count);
327:         if (taskCount > 2U)
328:         {
329:             taskCount = 2U;
330:         }
331:         while (taskCount > 0U)
332:         {
333:             DebugSerial_Task10ms();
334:             CarBase_Task10ms();
335:             taskCount--;
336:         }
337: 
338:         if (Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_100ms_count) > 0U)
339:         {
340:             CarBase_Task100ms();
341:         }
342: 
343:         if (Main_TakeTaskCounterAll(&g_task_200ms_count) > 0U)
344:         {
345:             CarBase_Task200ms();
346:         }
347:     }
348: 
349: #endif
350: }

16: static float H26_T5_AbsFloat(float value)
17: {
18:     return (value < 0.0f) ? -value : value;
19: }
20: 
21: static uint16_t H26_T5_AddMs(uint16_t value, uint16_t deltaMs)
22: {
23:     if (value > (uint16_t)(0xFFFFU - deltaMs))
24:     {
25:         return 0xFFFFU;
26:     }
27:     return (uint16_t)(value + deltaMs);
28: }
29: 
30: static void H26_T5_StopCar(void)
31: {
32:     App_Control_ForcePWMZero();
33: }
34: 
35: /* Reuse task 4 PID; add encoder acceleration feed-forward only while driving. */
36: static H26_BallControlSample_t H26_T5_UpdateBallControl(uint32_t nowMs,
37:                                                           uint8_t mode)
38: {
39:     float feedForwardTiltMm = 0.0f;
40:     float plannedFeedForwardTiltMm;
41:     float targetCm = H26_T5_O_TARGET_CM;
42:     float ballKp = H26_T5_BALL_STRAIGHT_KP_MM_PER_CM;
43:     float ballKi = H26_T5_BALL_STRAIGHT_KI_MM_PER_CM_S;
44:     float ballKd = H26_T5_BALL_STRAIGHT_KD_MM_PER_CMPS;
45:     uint32_t elapsedMs;
46: 
47:     plannedFeedForwardTiltMm = H26_T4_FF_TILT_SIGN_FOR_FORWARD_ACCEL *
48:         H26_T4_FF_K_MM_PER_CMPS2 * H26_T5_LAUNCH_ACCEL_CMPS2;
49:     if (plannedFeedForwardTiltMm > H26_T4_FF_TILT_LIMIT_MM)
50:     {
51:         plannedFeedForwardTiltMm = H26_T4_FF_TILT_LIMIT_MM;
52:     }
53: 
54:     if (mode == 2U)
55:     {
56:         /* Keep a calibrated lead position while the chassis is in motion. */
57:         targetCm += H26_T5_DRIVE_TARGET_COMPENSATION_CM;
58:         H26_BallControl_SetIntegralFrozen(
59:             ((nowMs - s_chassisStartMs) < H26_T5_INTEGRAL_FREEZE_MS) ?
60:             1U : 0U);
61:         feedForwardTiltMm = H26_BallControl_UpdateEncoderFeedForward(nowMs);
62:         elapsedMs = nowMs - s_chassisStartMs;
63:         if (elapsedMs < H26_T5_FF_HANDOFF_MS)
64:         {
65:             feedForwardTiltMm = plannedFeedForwardTiltMm +
66:                 (feedForwardTiltMm - plannedFeedForwardTiltMm) *
67:                 (float)elapsedMs / (float)H26_T5_FF_HANDOFF_MS;
68:         }
69:     }
70:     else if (mode == 1U)
71:     {
72:         H26_BallControl_SetIntegralFrozen(1U);
73:         feedForwardTiltMm = plannedFeedForwardTiltMm;
74:     }
75:     else
76:     {
77:         H26_BallControl_SetIntegralFrozen(0U);
78:     }
79: 
80:     if (mode == 2U && H26_Task2_IsCurveMode() != 0U)
81:     {
82:         ballKp = H26_T5_BALL_CURVE_KP_MM_PER_CM;
83:         ballKi = H26_T5_BALL_CURVE_KI_MM_PER_CM_S;
84:         ballKd = H26_T5_BALL_CURVE_KD_MM_PER_CMPS;
85:     }
86: 
87:     return H26_BallControl_Task10msWithPidFeedForward(nowMs,
88:         targetCm,
89:         ballKp,
90:         ballKi,
91:         ballKd,
92:         H26_T5_BALL_INTEGRAL_LIMIT_CM_S,
93:         H26_T5_BALL_TILT_COMMAND_LIMIT_MM,
94:         feedForwardTiltMm,
95:         H26_T5_BALL_POSITION_DEADBAND_CM);
96: }
97: 
98: static float H26_T5_GetChassisSpeedLimit(uint32_t nowMs)
99: {
100:     uint32_t elapsedMs;
101: 
102:     if (H26_T5_STRAIGHT_ACCEL_RAMP_MS == 0U)
103:     {
104:         return H26_T5_STRAIGHT_SPEED_CMPS;
105:     }
106: 
107:     elapsedMs = nowMs - s_chassisStartMs;
108:     if (elapsedMs >= H26_T5_STRAIGHT_ACCEL_RAMP_MS)
109:     {
110:         return H26_T5_STRAIGHT_SPEED_CMPS;
111:     }
112: 
113:     return H26_T5_STRAIGHT_SPEED_CMPS * (float)elapsedMs /
114:         (float)H26_T5_STRAIGHT_ACCEL_RAMP_MS;
115: }
116: 
117: static void H26_T5_EnterFault(H26_Task5Fault_t fault)
118: {
119:     H26_T5_StopCar();
120:     H26_Task2_ForceFault();
121:     H26_BallControl_Stop();
122:     s_fault = fault;
123:     s_state = H26_T5_FAULT;
124: }
125: 
126: static void H26_T5_UpdateBallPeak(H26_BallControlSample_t sample)
127: {
128:     float errorAbs;
129: 
130:     if (sample != H26_BALL_SAMPLE_NEW)
131:     {
132:         return;
133:     }
134: 
135:     errorAbs = H26_T5_AbsFloat(H26_BallControl_GetPositionCm());
136:     if (errorAbs > s_ballPeakErrorCm)
137:     {
138:         s_ballPeakErrorCm = errorAbs;
139:     }
140: }

142: void H26_Task5_Init(void)
143: {
144:     H26_Task5_Reset();
145: }
146: 
147: void H26_Task5_Reset(void)
148: {
149:     H26_T5_StopCar();
150:     H26_Task2_Reset();
151:     H26_BallControl_Reset();
152:     H26_BallControl_ResetEncoderFeedForward(0U);
153:     s_state = H26_T5_IDLE;
154:     s_fault = H26_T5_FAULT_NONE;
155:     s_startMs = 0U;
156:     s_chassisStartMs = 0U;
157:     s_finalElapsedMs = 0U;
158:     s_oAcquireHoldMs = 0U;
159:     s_ballPeakErrorCm = 0.0f;
160: }
161: 
162: void H26_Task5_Start(uint32_t startMs)
163: {
164:     H26_Task5_Reset();
165:     H26_BallControl_Start();
166:     H26_BallControl_ResetEncoderFeedForward(startMs);
167:     s_startMs = startMs;
168:     s_state = H26_T5_ACQUIRE_O;
169: }
170: 
171: void H26_Task5_ForceFault(void)
172: {
173:     H26_T5_EnterFault((s_fault == H26_T5_FAULT_NONE) ?
174:         H26_T5_FAULT_ILLEGAL_STATE : s_fault);
175: }
176: 
177: H26_Task5Result_t H26_Task5_Task10ms(uint32_t nowMs)
178: {
179:     H26_BallControlSample_t sample;
180:     H26_Task2Result_t chassisResult;
181: 
182:     switch (s_state)
183:     {
184:     case H26_T5_ACQUIRE_O:
185:         H26_T5_StopCar();
186:         sample = H26_T5_UpdateBallControl(nowMs, 0U);
187:         H26_T5_UpdateBallPeak(sample);
188:         if (sample == H26_BALL_SAMPLE_NEW &&
189:             H26_T5_AbsFloat(H26_BallControl_GetPositionCm()) <=
190:                 H26_T5_O_TOLERANCE_CM)
191:         {
192:             s_oAcquireHoldMs = H26_T5_AddMs(s_oAcquireHoldMs,
193:                 H26_BallControl_GetStableSampleMs());
194:             if (s_oAcquireHoldMs >= H26_T5_O_ACQUIRE_HOLD_MS)
195:             {
196:                 /* Prime the rod before the first non-zero traction command. */
197:                 s_chassisStartMs = nowMs;
198:                 s_state = H26_T5_PRETILT;
199:             }
200:         }
201:         else
202:         {
203:             s_oAcquireHoldMs = 0U;
204:         }
205:         break;
206: 
207:     case H26_T5_PRETILT:
208:         H26_T5_StopCar();
209:         sample = H26_T5_UpdateBallControl(nowMs, 1U);
210:         H26_T5_UpdateBallPeak(sample);
211:         if ((nowMs - s_chassisStartMs) >= H26_T5_PRETILT_MS)
212:         {
213:             /* Shared chassis machine with task-2 line-following parameters. */
214:             s_chassisStartMs = nowMs;
215:             H26_Task2_StartForTask5(s_chassisStartMs);
216:             H26_BallControl_ResetEncoderFeedForward(nowMs);
217:             s_state = H26_T5_LEAVE_A;
218:         }
219:         break;
220: 
221:     case H26_T5_LEAVE_A:
222:     case H26_T5_LAP_RUNNING:
223:         sample = H26_T5_UpdateBallControl(nowMs, 2U);
224:         H26_T5_UpdateBallPeak(sample);
225:         H26_Task2_SetForwardSpeedLimit(H26_T5_GetChassisSpeedLimit(nowMs));
226:         chassisResult = H26_Task2_Task10ms(nowMs);
227: 
228:         if (H26_Task2_GetState() == H26_T2_LAP_RUNNING)
229:         {
230:             s_state = H26_T5_LAP_RUNNING;
231:         }
232:         if (chassisResult == H26_T2_RESULT_FINISHED)
233:         {
234:             if (s_finalElapsedMs == 0U)
235:             {
236:                 s_finalElapsedMs = H26_Task2_GetFinalElapsedMs();
237:             }
238:             s_state = H26_T5_DONE;
239:             return H26_T5_RESULT_FINISHED;
240:         }
241:         if (chassisResult == H26_T2_RESULT_FAULT)
242:         {
243:             H26_T5_EnterFault(H26_T5_FAULT_ILLEGAL_STATE);
244:             return H26_T5_RESULT_FAULT;
245:         }
246:         if (s_finalElapsedMs == 0U &&
247:             H26_Task2_GetState() == H26_T2_BRAKING)
248:         {
249:             /* A is confirmed: freeze task-5 time while task-2 tracks out. */
250:             s_finalElapsedMs = nowMs - s_startMs;
251:         }
252:         break;
253: 
254:     case H26_T5_DONE:
255:         H26_T5_StopCar();
256:         return H26_T5_RESULT_FINISHED;
257: 
258:     case H26_T5_FAULT:
259:         H26_T5_StopCar();
260:         H26_BallControl_Stop();
261:         return H26_T5_RESULT_FAULT;
262: 
263:     case H26_T5_IDLE:
264:     default:
265:         H26_T5_EnterFault(H26_T5_FAULT_ILLEGAL_STATE);
266:         return H26_T5_RESULT_FAULT;
267:     }
268: 
269:     return H26_T5_RESULT_RUNNING;
270: }
271: 
272: void H26_Task5_HoldBall10ms(uint32_t nowMs)
273: {
274:     H26_BallControlSample_t sample = H26_T5_UpdateBallControl(nowMs, 0U);
275:     H26_T5_UpdateBallPeak(sample);
276: }
277: 
278: H26_Task5State_t H26_Task5_GetState(void) { return s_state; }
279: H26_Task5Fault_t H26_Task5_GetFault(void) { return s_fault; }
280: 
281: uint32_t H26_Task5_GetElapsedMs(uint32_t nowMs)
282: {
283:     if (s_state == H26_T5_IDLE)
284:     {
285:         return 0U;
286:     }
287:     if (s_finalElapsedMs != 0U || s_state == H26_T5_DONE)
288:     {
289:         return s_finalElapsedMs;
290:     }
291:     return nowMs - s_startMs;

81:     App_BallLink_Reset();
82:     RodEncoder_Reset();
83: }
84: 
85: static uint8_t H26_IsTaskIdValid(H26_TaskId_t task)
86: {
87:     return (task >= H26_TASK_2 && task <= H26_TASK_6) ? 1U : 0U;
88: }
89: 
90: static H26_LedMode_t H26_GetStateLedRestoreMode(void)
91: {
92:     if (s_systemState == H26_SYS_STOPPED || s_systemState == H26_SYS_FAULT)
93:     {
94:         return H26_LED_FAULT;
95:     }
96:     if (s_systemState == H26_SYS_FINISHED)
97:     {
98:         return H26_LED_FINISHED;
99:     }
100:     return H26_LED_OFF;
101: }
102: 
103: static void H26_ShowSelectedTask(void)
104: {
105:     H26_LedShowTask((uint8_t)s_selectedTask, H26_GetStateLedRestoreMode());
106: }
107: 
108: static void H26_EnterFault(const char *reason)
109: {
110:     H26_SafeStop();
111:     H26_Task2_ForceFault();
112:     H26_Task3_ForceFault();
113:     H26_Task4_ForceFault();
114:     H26_Task5_ForceFault();
115:     s_systemState = H26_SYS_FAULT;
116:     H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_FAULT);
117:     DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,fault,%s]\r\n", reason);
118: }
119: 
120: static void H26_SelectNextTask(void)
121: {
122:     if (s_selectedTask >= H26_TASK_6)
123:     {
124:         s_selectedTask = H26_TASK_2;
125:     }
126:     else
127:     {
128:         s_selectedTask = (H26_TaskId_t)((uint8_t)s_selectedTask + 1U);
129:     }
130: 
131:     if (s_systemState == H26_SYS_FINISHED)
132:     {
133:         H26_ClearRunData();
134:         s_systemState = H26_SYS_SELECT;
135:     }
136:     else
137:     {
138:         H26_SafeStop();
139:     }
140: 
141:     H26_ShowSelectedTask();
142:     DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,select,task=%u]\r\n", (unsigned int)s_selectedTask);
143: }
144: 
145: static void H26_StartCurrentTask(uint8_t task4DriveMode)
146: {
147:     uint32_t nowMs;
148: 
149:     if (H26_IsTaskIdValid(s_selectedTask) == 0U)
150:     {
151:         H26_EnterFault("task-id");
152:         return;
153:     }
154: 
155:     if (s_selectedTask != H26_TASK_2 && s_selectedTask != H26_TASK_3 &&
156:         s_selectedTask != H26_TASK_4 && s_selectedTask != H26_TASK_5)
157:     {
158:         H26_ClearRunData();
159:         s_systemState = H26_SYS_SELECT;
160:         H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_OFF);
161:         H26_ShowSelectedTask();
162:         DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,task=%u,not-implemented]\r\n",
163:                            (unsigned int)s_selectedTask);
164:         return;
165:     }
166: 
167:     H26_SafeStop();
168:     H26_ClearEncoderAndControlData();
169:     nowMs = Timer_GetMillis();
170:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_2)
171:     {
172:         H26_Task2_Start(nowMs);
173:     }
174:     else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
175:     {
176:         H26_Task3_Start(nowMs);
177:     }
178:     else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
179:     {
180:         if (task4DriveMode != 0U)
181:         {
182:             H26_Task4_StartDrive(nowMs);
183:         }
184:         else
185:         {
186:             H26_Task4_Start(nowMs);
187:         }
188:     }
189:     else
190:     {
191:         H26_Task5_Start(nowMs);
192:     }
193:     s_systemState = H26_SYS_PREPARE;
194:     H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_RUNNING);
195:     DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,start,task=%u,mode=%u,ms=%lu]\r\n",
196:         (unsigned int)s_selectedTask, (unsigned int)task4DriveMode,
197:         (unsigned long)nowMs);
198: }
199: 
200: void H26_Init(void)
201: {
202:     s_selectedTask = H26_TASK_2;
203:     s_systemState = H26_SYS_SELECT;
204:     H26_Task2_Init();
205:     H26_Task3_Init();
206:     H26_Task4_Init();
207:     H26_Task5_Init();
208:     App_BallLink_Init();
209:     H26_ClearRunData();
210:     H26_LedInit();
211:     H26_ShowSelectedTask();
212:     DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,init,task=2]\r\n");
213: }
214: 
215: void H26_Tick1ms(void)
216: {
217:     H26_LedTick1ms();
218: }
219: 
220: void H26_KeyProcess(void)
221: {
222:     uint8_t key = Key_GetNum();
223: 
224:     if (key == 0U)
225:     {
226:         return;
227:     }
228: 
229:     if (key == 3U)
230:     {
231:         if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4 &&
232:             (s_systemState == H26_SYS_SELECT ||
233:              s_systemState == H26_SYS_FINISHED))
234:         {
235:             H26_StartCurrentTask(0U);
236:             return;
237:         }
238: 
239:         H26_SafeStop();
240:         s_systemState = H26_SYS_STOPPED;
241:         H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_FAULT);
242:         DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,manual-stop]\r\n");
243:         return;
244:     }
245: 
246:     if (key == 4U)
247:     {
248:         H26_ClearRunData();
249:         s_systemState = H26_SYS_SELECT;
250:         H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_OFF);
251:         H26_ShowSelectedTask();
252:         DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,reset,task=%u]\r\n", (unsigned int)s_selectedTask);
253:         return;
254:     }
255: 
256:     if (key == 1U)
257:     {
258:         if (s_systemState == H26_SYS_SELECT ||
259:             s_systemState == H26_SYS_FINISHED ||
260:             s_systemState == H26_SYS_STOPPED)
261:         {
262:             H26_SelectNextTask();
263:         }
264:         return;
265:     }
266: 
267:     if (key == 2U &&
268:         (s_systemState == H26_SYS_SELECT || s_systemState == H26_SYS_FINISHED))
269:     {
270:         H26_StartCurrentTask((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4) ? 1U : 0U);
271:     }
272: }
273: 
274: void H26_Task10ms(void)
275: {
276:     H26_Task2Result_t task2Result;
277:     H26_Task3Result_t task3Result;
278:     H26_Task4Result_t task4Result;
279:     H26_Task5Result_t task5Result;
280:     uint32_t nowMs = Timer_GetMillis();
281: 
282:     switch (s_systemState)
283:     {
284:     case H26_SYS_SELECT:
285:     case H26_SYS_STOPPED:
286:     case H26_SYS_FAULT:
287:         H26_SafeStop();
288:         break;
289: 
290:     case H26_SYS_FINISHED:
291:         if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
292:         {
293:             /* Task 3 has completed its final +5 cm PID settle. */
294:             H26_SafeCarStop();
295:             task3Result = H26_Task3_Task10ms(nowMs);
296:             if (task3Result == H26_T3_RESULT_FAULT)
297:             {
298:                 H26_EnterFault("task3-hold");
299:             }
300:         }
301:         else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
302:         {
303:             H26_SafeCarStop();
304:             H26_Task4_HoldBall10ms(nowMs);
305:         }
306:         else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5)
307:         {
308:             /* Lap time is frozen; retain active O-point ball stabilization. */
309:             H26_SafeCarStop();
310:             H26_Task5_HoldBall10ms(nowMs);
311:         }
312:         else
313:         {
314:             H26_SafeStop();
315:         }
316:         break;
317: 
318:     case H26_SYS_PREPARE:
319:         H26_SafeStop();
320:         if ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_2 &&
321:              H26_Task2_GetState() == H26_T2_LEAVE_A) ||
322:             (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3 &&
323:              H26_Task3_GetState() == H26_T3_READY) ||
324:              (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4 &&
325:                (H26_Task4_GetState() == H26_T4_MANUAL_MOVE_HOLD_O ||
326:                 H26_Task4_GetState() == H26_T4_DRIVE_ACQUIRE_O)) ||
327:             (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5 &&
328:              H26_Task5_GetState() == H26_T5_ACQUIRE_O))
329:         {
330:             s_systemState = H26_SYS_RUNNING;
331:         }
332:         else
333:         {
334:             H26_EnterFault("prepare");
335:         }
336:         break;
337: 
338:     case H26_SYS_RUNNING:
339:         if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_2)
340:         {
341:             task2Result = H26_Task2_Task10ms(nowMs);
342:             if (task2Result == H26_T2_RESULT_FINISHED)
343:             {
344:                 H26_SafeStop();
345:                 s_systemState = H26_SYS_FINISHED;
346:                 H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_FINISHED);
347:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,finish,task=2,ms=%lu]\r\n",
348:                     (unsigned long)H26_Task2_GetFinalElapsedMs());
349:             }
350:             else if (task2Result == H26_T2_RESULT_FAULT)
351:             {
352:                 H26_EnterFault("task2");
353:             }
354:         }
355:         else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
356:         {
357:             /* Task 3 owns only the rod stepper; vehicle traction stays forced off. */
358:             H26_SafeCarStop();
359:             task3Result = H26_Task3_Task10ms(nowMs);
360:             if (task3Result == H26_T3_RESULT_FINISHED)
361:             {
362:                 H26_SafeCarStop();
363:                 s_systemState = H26_SYS_FINISHED;
364:                 H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_FINISHED);
365:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[t3f,%lu]\r\n",
366:                     (unsigned long)H26_Task3_GetFinalElapsedMs());
367:             }
368:             else if (task3Result == H26_T3_RESULT_FAULT)
369:             {
370:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[t3e,%u]\r\n",
371:                     (unsigned int)H26_Task3_GetFault());
372:                 H26_EnterFault("task3");
373:             }
374:         }
375:         else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
376:         {
377:             task4Result = H26_Task4_Task10ms(nowMs);
378:             if (task4Result == H26_T4_RESULT_FINISHED)
379:             {
380:                 H26_SafeStop();
381:                 s_systemState = H26_SYS_FINISHED;
382:                 H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_FINISHED);
383:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,finish,task=4]\r\n");
384:             }
385:             else if (task4Result == H26_T4_RESULT_FAULT)
386:             {
387:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,task4-fault,code=%u]\r\n",
388:                     (unsigned int)H26_Task4_GetFault());
389:                 H26_EnterFault("task4");
390:             }
391:         }
392:         else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5)
393:         {
394:             task5Result = H26_Task5_Task10ms(nowMs);
395:             if (task5Result == H26_T5_RESULT_FINISHED)
396:             {
397:                 H26_SafeStop();
398:                 s_systemState = H26_SYS_FINISHED;
399:                 H26_LedSetMode(H26_LED_FINISHED);
400:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,finish,task=5,ms=%lu,peak_c=%ld]\r\n",
401:                     (unsigned long)H26_Task5_GetFinalElapsedMs(),
402:                     (long)(H26_Task5_GetBallPeakErrorCm() * 100.0f));
403:             }
404:             else if (task5Result == H26_T5_RESULT_FAULT)
405:             {
406:                 DebugSerial_Printf("[h26,task5-fault,code=%u]\r\n",
407:                     (unsigned int)H26_Task5_GetFault());
408:                 H26_EnterFault("task5");
409:             }
410:         }
411:         else
412:         {
413:             H26_EnterFault("run-task");
414:         }
415:         break;
416: 
417:     default:
418:         H26_EnterFault("sys-state");
419:         break;
420:     }
421: }
422: 
423: void H26_Task100ms(void)
424: {
425: #if !H26_VERBOSE_TELEMETRY_ENABLE
426:     static uint16_t debugMs = 0U;
427: 
428:     debugMs = (debugMs > (uint16_t)(0xFFFFU - 100U)) ? 0xFFFFU :
429:         (uint16_t)(debugMs + 100U);
430:     if (debugMs < H26_DEBUG_PERIOD_MS)
431:     {
432:         return;
433:     }
434:     debugMs = 0U;
435: 
436:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
437:     {
438:         DebugSerial_Printf(
439:             "[t3,%u,%u,%lu,%lu,%u]\r\n",
440:             (unsigned int)s_systemState,
441:             (unsigned int)H26_Task3_GetState(),
442:             (unsigned long)H26_Task3_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis()),
443:             (unsigned long)H26_Task3_GetFinalElapsedMs(),
444:             (unsigned int)H26_Task3_GetFault());
445:         return;
446:     }
447: 
448:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
449:     {
450:         DebugSerial_Printf(
451:             "[h26,t4=%u,p=%ld,e=%ld,ff=%ld,t=%lu,f=%u]\r\n",
452:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_GetState(),
453:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetBallPositionCm() * 100.0f),
454:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetBallErrorCm() * 100.0f),
455:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetFeedForwardTiltMm() * 1000.0f),
456:             (unsigned long)H26_Task4_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis()),
457:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_GetFault());
458:         return;
459:     }
460: 
461:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5)
462:     {
463:         DebugSerial_Printf(
464:             "[h26,sys=%u,task=5,t5=%u,dist_c=%ld,pos_c=%ld,peak_c=%ld,elapsed=%lu,final=%lu,fault=%u]\r\n",
465:             (unsigned int)s_systemState,
466:             (unsigned int)H26_Task5_GetState(),
467:             (long)(H26_Task5_GetDistanceCm() * 100.0f),
468:             (long)(H26_Task5_GetBallPositionCm() * 100.0f),
469:             (long)(H26_Task5_GetBallPeakErrorCm() * 100.0f),
470:             (unsigned long)H26_Task5_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis()),
471:             (unsigned long)H26_Task5_GetFinalElapsedMs(),
472:             (unsigned int)H26_Task5_GetFault());
473:         return;
474:     }
475: 
476:     DebugSerial_Printf(
477:         "[h26,sys=%u,task=2,t2=%u,dist_c=%ld,cmd_v_c=%ld,mask=%02X,black=%u,elapsed=%lu,final=%lu]\r\n",
478:         (unsigned int)s_systemState,
479:         (unsigned int)H26_Task2_GetState(),
480:         (long)(H26_Task2_GetDistanceCm() * 100.0f),
481:         (long)(H26_Task2_GetCommandForwardSpeed() * 100.0f),
482:         (unsigned int)g_lineMask,
483:         (unsigned int)g_lineBlackCount,
484:         (unsigned long)H26_Task2_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis()),
485:         (unsigned long)H26_Task2_GetFinalElapsedMs());
486: #else
487:     static uint16_t debugMs = 0U;
488:     uint32_t elapsedMs;
489:     int32_t distanceCentiCm;
490:     int32_t leftSpeedCenti;
491:     int32_t rightSpeedCenti;
492:     int32_t commandSpeedCenti;
493: 
494:     debugMs = (debugMs > (uint16_t)(0xFFFFU - 100U)) ? 0xFFFFU :
495:         (uint16_t)(debugMs + 100U);
496:     if (debugMs < H26_DEBUG_PERIOD_MS)
497:     {
498:         return;
499:     }
500:     debugMs = 0U;
501: 
502:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
503:     {
504:         DebugSerial_Printf(
505:             "[t3,%u,%u,%lu,%lu,%u]\r\n",
506:             (unsigned int)s_systemState,
507:             (unsigned int)H26_Task3_GetState(),
508:             (unsigned long)H26_Task3_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis()),
509:             (unsigned long)H26_Task3_GetFinalElapsedMs(),
510:             (unsigned int)H26_Task3_GetFault());
511:         return;
512:     }
513: 
514:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
515:     {
516:         elapsedMs = H26_Task4_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis());
517: 
518:         DebugSerial_Printf(
519:             "[h26,sys=%u,task=4,t4=%u,dist_c=%ld,cmd_v_c=%ld,turn_c=%ld,pos_c=%ld,err_c=%ld,vel_c=%ld,car_v_c=%ld,car_a_c=%ld,pid_um=%ld,ff_um=%ld,tilt_um=%ld,"
520:             "rod=%ld,rod_t=%ld,vis=%u,org=%u,conf=%u,elapsed=%lu,fault=%u]\r\n",
521:             (unsigned int)s_systemState,
522:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_GetState(),
523:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetDistanceCm() * 100.0f),
524:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetCommandForwardSpeedCmps() * 100.0f),
525:             (long)(g_targetTurnSpeed * 100.0f),
526:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetBallPositionCm() * 100.0f),
527:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetBallErrorCm() * 100.0f),
528:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetBallSpeedCmps() * 100.0f),
529:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetForwardSpeedCmps() * 100.0f),
530:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetForwardAccelerationCmps2() * 100.0f),
531:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetPidTiltCommandMm() * 1000.0f),
532:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetFeedForwardTiltMm() * 1000.0f),
533:             (long)(H26_Task4_GetTiltCommandMm() * 1000.0f),
534:             (long)H26_Task4_GetRodEncoderCount(),
535:             (long)H26_Task4_GetRodTargetCount(),
536:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_IsVisionValid(),
537:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_IsOriginCalibrated(),
538:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_GetVisionConfidence(),
539:             (unsigned long)elapsedMs,
540:             (unsigned int)H26_Task4_GetFault());
541:         return;
542:     }
543: 
544:     elapsedMs = H26_Task2_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis());
545:     distanceCentiCm = (int32_t)(H26_Task2_GetDistanceCm() * 100.0f);
546:     leftSpeedCenti = (int32_t)(g_leftSpeed * 100.0f);
547:     rightSpeedCenti = (int32_t)(g_rightSpeed * 100.0f);
548:     commandSpeedCenti = (int32_t)(H26_Task2_GetCommandForwardSpeed() * 100.0f);
549: 
550:     DebugSerial_Printf(
551:         "[h26,s=%u,task=%u,t2=%u,curve=%u,v_c=%ld,mask=%02X,black=%u,"
552:         "exit=%u,finish=%u,dist_c=%ld,t=%lu,vl_c=%ld,vr_c=%ld]\r\n",
553:         (unsigned int)s_systemState,
554:         (unsigned int)s_selectedTask,
555:         (unsigned int)H26_Task2_GetState(),
556:         (unsigned int)H26_Task2_IsCurveMode(),
557:         (long)commandSpeedCenti,
558:         (unsigned int)g_lineMask,
559:         (unsigned int)g_lineBlackCount,
560:         (unsigned int)H26_Task2_IsFinishEnabled(),
561:         (unsigned int)H26_Task2_IsFinishLatched(),
562:         (long)distanceCentiCm,
563:         (unsigned long)elapsedMs,
564:         (long)leftSpeedCenti,
565:         (long)rightSpeedCenti);
566: #endif
567: }
568: 
569: void H26_Task200ms(void)
570: {
571: #if CAR_OLED_ENABLE
572:     uint32_t elapsedMs;
573:     uint32_t seconds;
574:     uint32_t centiseconds;
575: 
576:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
577:     {
578:         elapsedMs = H26_Task3_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis());
579:     }
580:     else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
581:     {
582:         elapsedMs = H26_Task4_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis());
583:     }
584:     else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5)
585:     {
586:         elapsedMs = H26_Task5_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis());
587:     }
588:     else
589:     {
590:         elapsedMs = H26_Task2_GetElapsedMs(Timer_GetMillis());
591:     }
592:     seconds = elapsedMs / 1000U;
593:     centiseconds = (elapsedMs % 1000U) / 10U;
594: 
595:     OLED_Clear();
596:     if (s_systemState == H26_SYS_FINISHED)
597:     {
598:         OLED_ShowString(0, 0, (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3) ?
599:                         "TASK 3 FINISH" :
600:                         ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4) ? "TASK 4 FINISH" :
601:                          ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5) ? "TASK 5 FINISH" :
602:                           "TASK 2 FINISH")), OLED_6X8);
603:         OLED_ShowString(0, 16, "STOPPED", OLED_6X8);
604:     }
605:     else
606:     {
607:         OLED_ShowString(0, 0, "TASK ", OLED_6X8);
608:         OLED_ShowNum(36, 0, (uint32_t)s_selectedTask, 1, OLED_6X8);
609:         OLED_ShowString(0, 16, "SYS:", OLED_6X8);
610:         OLED_ShowNum(24, 16, (uint32_t)s_systemState, 1, OLED_6X8);
611:         OLED_ShowString(48, 16, (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3) ? "T3:" :
612:                         ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4) ? "T4:" :
613:                          ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5) ? "T5:" : "T2:")), OLED_6X8);
614:         OLED_ShowNum(66, 16,
615:             (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3) ? (uint32_t)H26_Task3_GetState() :
616:             ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4) ? (uint32_t)H26_Task4_GetState() :
617:              ((s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_5) ? (uint32_t)H26_Task5_GetState() :
618:               (uint32_t)H26_Task2_GetState())), 1, OLED_6X8);
619:     }
620:     OLED_ShowString(0, 32, "TIME:", OLED_6X8);
621:     OLED_ShowNum(36, 32, seconds, 2, OLED_6X8);
622:     OLED_ShowString(48, 32, ".", OLED_6X8);
623:     OLED_ShowNum(54, 32, centiseconds, 2, OLED_6X8);
624:     OLED_ShowString(72, 32, "s", OLED_6X8);
625:     if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_3)
626:     {
627:         OLED_ShowString(0, 48, "FINAL PID +5", OLED_6X8);
628:     }
629:     else if (s_selectedTask == H26_TASK_4)
630:     {
631:         OLED_ShowString(0, 48, "P:", OLED_6X8);
632:         H26_OledShowSignedCentiCm(12, 48,
633:             (int32_t)(H26_Task4_GetBallPositionCm() * 100.0f));

1: # mspm0_car_base
2: 
3: MSPM0G3507 小车底层驱动模板工程。保留 MSPM0G3507 小车底层驱动、IO 映射、速度闭环、灰度读取、串口、按键、OLED、JY61P、舵机、板级测试能力。

19: | 编码器每厘米计数 | `ECAR_CM_PER_PULSE` | 见下方各模式 | 取决于编码器类型和计数方式 |

38: | 转角 | 编码器计数 | STEP 脉冲 | 说明 |

66: ## 软件结构
67: 
68: | 目录 | 说明 |
69: | --- | --- |
70: | `User/` | 主入口与主循环 |
71: | `App/` | app_control(速度闭环)、app_line(循迹)、app_board_test(板测)、app_car_base(模板入口)、app_car_state(全局运行变量) |
72: | `Control/` | PID |
73: | `Hardware/` | MSPM0G3507 外设驱动封装 |
74: | `System/` | 系统节拍、定时器、调度 |
75: | `keil/` | Keil MDK 工程文件 |
76: 
77: 板级逻辑名集中在 `Hardware/Board_Config.h`。
78: 
79: ## 安全默认状态
80: 
81: - **上电默认电机不转**：PWM 默认 0，方向脚安全状态。
82: - **`g_carEnable` 默认 0**：不使能电机输出。
83: - **不自动启动任何比赛任务**：main 入口使用 `CarBase_Task10ms()` 空任务，不会自动进入任何竞赛状态机。
84: 
85: ## IO 映射
86: 
87: ### D36A 步进电机驱动（从车）
88: 
89: | 逻辑名 | 硬件连接 | 说明 |
90: | --- | --- | --- |
91: | `STEP_L` | PB15 / TIMG7 CC0 | 左通道 STEP，硬件 50% 占空比脉冲 |
92: | `DIR_L` | PB18 | 左通道方向，高电平=物理右轮前进 |
93: | `STEP_R` | PB16 / TIMG8 CC1 | 右通道 STEP，硬件 50% 占空比脉冲 |
94: | `DIR_R` | PB25 | 右通道方向，低电平=物理左轮前进 |
95: | EN1/EN2 | 硬件常态使能 | 不占用 MCU IO |
96: 
97: > **通道交叉映射**：D36A 接线中 STEP_L/DIR_L 实际驱动物理右轮，STEP_R/DIR_R 实际驱动物理左轮。方向宏 `LEFT_STEPPER_DIR_SIGN = +1`, `RIGHT_STEPPER_DIR_SIGN = -1`。
98: 
99: ### TB6612 电机驱动（主车/旧版）
100: 
101: | 逻辑名 | 硬件连接 | 说明 |
102: | --- | --- | --- |
103: | `MOTOR_L_PWM` | TIMG0-C0 | 左电机 PWM |
104: | `MOTOR_L_IN1` | B17 | 左电机方向 1 |
105: | `MOTOR_L_IN2` | B19 | 左电机方向 2 |
106: | `MOTOR_R_PWM` | TIMG0-C1 | 右电机 PWM |
107: | `MOTOR_R_IN1` | A16 | 右电机方向 1 |
108: | `MOTOR_R_IN2` | B24 | 右电机方向 2 |
109: | `MOTOR_STBY` | 5V | 常使能，不占用 MCU IO |
110: 
111: STBY 直接接 5V，软件只能通过 PWM=0 和方向脚安全状态停车。
112: 
113: ### 编码器
114: 
115: **步进模式（从车，4× 正交解码，GPIOB）：**
116: 
117: | 逻辑名 | 引脚 | 说明 |
118: | --- | --- | --- |
119: | `ENC_L_A` | PB05 | 左编码器 A 相，双边沿中断 |
120: | `ENC_L_B` | PB12 | 左编码器 B 相，双边沿中断 |
121: | `ENC_R_A` | PB08 | 右编码器 A 相，双边沿中断 |
122: | `ENC_R_B` | PB00 | 右编码器 B 相，双边沿中断 |
123: 
124: > 16 项查表法 4× 正交解码，4096 计数/圈。非法跳变计数支持丢步检测。
125: 
126: **直流电机模式（主车，A 相上升沿计数，GPIOA）：**
127: 
128: | 逻辑名 | 引脚 | 说明 |
129: | --- | --- | --- |
130: | `ENC_L_A` | PA26 | 左电机 A 相中断 |
131: | `ENC_L_B` | PA27 | 左电机 B 相方向采样 |
132: | `ENC_R_A` | PA25 | 右电机 A 相中断 |
133: | `ENC_R_B` | PA14 | 右电机 B 相方向采样 |
134: 
135: > A 相上升沿计数 + B 相电平判断方向，367 计数/圈。
136: 
137: ### 八路灰度（CD4051 轮询）
138: 
139: | 逻辑名 | 引脚 | 说明 |
140: | --- | --- | --- |
141: | `GRAY_AD2` | PB23 | 地址位 |
142: | `GRAY_AD1` | PB10 | 地址位 |
143: | `GRAY_AD0` | PB13 | 地址位 |
144: | `GRAY_OUT` | PB01 | 灰度输出（需硬件 5V->3.3V 电平转换） |
145: 
146: ### 其他外设
147: 
148: | 外设 | 映射 |
149: | --- | --- |
150: | OLED (I2C, H8) | SCL=PB9, SDA=PB8, 4针 IIC SSD1306 |
151: | I2C0（预留） | PA31 SCL / PA28 SDA |
152: | JY61P | UART0, TX=PA0, RX=PA1, 9600 8N1 |
153: | BEEP | A07 |
154: | LED_USER | B04 |
155: | KEY1~KEY4 | B14 / B11 / B27 / B26, 输入上拉 |
156: | SERVO1~4 | PA21 / PA22 / PA15 / PA17 (TIMA0-C0~C3) |
157: | UART_DEBUG | UART1, TX1=PB6, RX1=PB7, 9600 8N1 |
158: | UART2/UART3 | 备用 |
159: 
160: **电机方向和编码器方向保持原样，本次未做修改。**
161: 
162: ## 板级测试模式
163: 
164: ```c
165: #define ECAR_BOARD_TEST_MODE              1
166: #define ECAR_TEST_RADIO_ENABLE            0
167: #define ECAR_TEST_STEPPER_ENCODER_ENABLE   0
168: #define ECAR_TEST_JY61P_ENABLE             1
169: ```
170: 
171: 以上配置用于 JY61P 板级测试；仓库默认仍为 `ECAR_BOARD_TEST_MODE = 0`、`ECAR_TEST_JY61P_ENABLE = 0`，不会进入板测或自动启动电机。板测子模式互斥，只能同时开启一个。
172: 
173: JY61P 接线：
174: 
175: - JY61P TX -> PA1 / UART0_RX。
176: - JY61P RX -> PA0 / UART0_TX。
177: - JY61P 与 MSPM0 必须共地，供电按实际模块要求连接。
178: - 上电前确认 UART 逻辑电平与 MSPM0 的 3.3V IO 兼容；不允许把 5V UART 信号直接送入 MSPM0。
179: 
180: JY61P 板测按键：
181: 
182: - K1：软件航向角归零；角度帧过期时拒绝归零。
183: - K2：暂停/恢复周期串口打印。
184: - K3：切换角度页/诊断页。
185: - K4：清除帧数与错误统计，不清除姿态数据、零点和新鲜度时间戳。
186: 
187: 驱动内部角度单位为 0.01°，角速度单位为 0.1°/s，均使用定点整数。JY61P 六轴 yaw 由陀螺仪积分得到，会随时间漂移，适合短时间转角、90°/180° 转弯和短期航向修正，不适合长期绝对航向定位。
188: 
189: ## CarBase 模板入口
190: 
191: - `CarBase_Init()`：安全初始化，清零 PWM，停止电机。
192: - `CarBase_Task10ms()`：空任务（仅 `App_Control_ForcePWMZero()`），保证上电电机不转。
193: - `CarBase_Task100ms()`：串口输出 base 状态（编码器、灰度）。
194: - `CarBase_Task200ms()`：OLED 占位显示。
195: - `CarBase_KeyProcess()`：空实现。
196: 
197: ## 开发新题目指南
198: 
199: 1. 在 `App/` 层新建业务状态机文件（如 `app_my_contest.c/h`）。
200: 2. 在 `app_car_base.h` 中声明新接口，或创建独立的 entry 文件。
201: 3. 将新文件加入 Keil 工程 App 组。
202: 4. 修改 `main.c`，将 `CarBase_Task10ms()` 替换为新的业务入口。
203: 5. **不修改底层驱动。**
204: 
205: ## 2021 F 题基础循迹框架
206: 
207: 当前在 `App/app_21f_car.c` 中实现了 2021 电赛 F 题"智能送药小车"基础去程循迹框架。
208: 
209: ### 按键功能
210: 
211: | 按键 | 功能 |
212: | --- | --- |
213: | K1 | 选择目标病房号 1~8 循环，LED 三位二进制提示房号 |
214: | K2 | 启动送药任务 |
215: | K3 | 紧急停车 |
216: | K4 | 复位到初始状态 |
217: 
218: ### 病房路线
219: 
220: - **1~4 号**：单路口单转弯（SIMPLE 路线），1/2号入口就近路口，3/4号远端路口。
221: - **5~8 号**：双路口双转弯（FAR 路线），主线 240cm 后第一次路口，转弯后 70cm 第二次路口。
222: - **当前返回逻辑未实现**，到达病房后等待 3 秒进入 FINISH。
223: 
224: ### 重要参数
225: 
226: - `F21_TURN_90_PULSE = 0`：**需要实测后填写**。当前为 0 时，转弯状态会安全停车并串口输出 `[f21,fault,turn90_not_set]`。
227: - `F21_LINE_BASE_SPEED_CMPS = 10.0`：直线循迹基础速度。
228: - `F21_TURN_PWM = 140`：转弯 PWM。
229: 
230: ### LED 房号提示
231: 
232: 非阻塞三位二进制显示：500ms 亮 = 1，100ms 亮 = 0，位间 100ms 灭。
233: 
234: ## SysConfig 复核项
235: 
236: 重新打开 SysConfig 或重新生成代码前，请复核：
237: 1. TIMG0-C0/C1 为电机 PWM。
238: 2. TIMA0-C0~C3 为舵机接口。
239: 3. 编码器 PA26/PA27/PA25/PA14 与实际接线一致。
240: 4. UART_DEBUG = UART1 PB6/PB7，9600 8N1。
241: 5. 灰度 GRAY_OUT 到 PB01 前已硬件限压到 3.3V。
242: 
243: ## 上车前安全检查
244: 
245: 1. 架空车轮后再打开电机测试宏。
246: 2. 确认 `g_carEnable` 默认 0，目标速度默认 0。
247: 3. 舵机测试前确认独立供电和共地。
248: 4. 灰度 5V 供电时需电平转换。

1: #include "app_h26_task3.h"

4: #include "RodStepper.h"
5: #include <stdint.h>
6: 
7: static volatile H26_Task3State_t s_state = H26_T3_IDLE;
8: static volatile H26_Task3Fault_t s_fault = H26_T3_FAULT_NONE;
9: static volatile uint32_t s_startMs = 0U;
10: static volatile uint32_t s_finalElapsedMs = 0U;
11: static volatile uint32_t s_phaseStartMs = 0U;
12: static volatile uint16_t s_stableMs = 0U;
13: 
14: static float H26_T3_AbsFloat(float value)
15: {
16:     return (value < 0.0f) ? -value : value;
17: }
18: 
19: static uint16_t H26_T3_AddMs(uint16_t value, uint16_t deltaMs)
20: {
21:     return (value > (uint16_t)(0xFFFFU - deltaMs)) ? 0xFFFFU :
22:         (uint16_t)(value + deltaMs);
23: }
24: 
25: static RodStepperDirection_t H26_T3_GetExtendDirection(void)
26: {
27:     return (H26_T3_CHAIN_EXTEND_DIR_POSITIVE != 0U) ?
28:         ROD_STEPPER_DIR_POSITIVE : ROD_STEPPER_DIR_NEGATIVE;
29: }
30: 
31: static RodStepperDirection_t H26_T3_GetRetractDirection(void)
32: {
33:     return (H26_T3_CHAIN_EXTEND_DIR_POSITIVE != 0U) ?
34:         ROD_STEPPER_DIR_NEGATIVE : ROD_STEPPER_DIR_POSITIVE;
35: }
36: 
37: static void H26_T3_EnterFault(H26_Task3Fault_t fault)
38: {
39:     H26_BallControl_Stop();
40:     s_fault = fault;
41:     s_state = H26_T3_FAULT;
42: }
43: 
44: void H26_Task3_Init(void)
45: {
46:     H26_Task3_Reset();
47: }
48: 
49: void H26_Task3_Reset(void)
50: {
51:     H26_BallControl_Reset();
52:     s_state = H26_T3_IDLE;
53:     s_fault = H26_T3_FAULT_NONE;
54:     s_startMs = 0U;
55:     s_finalElapsedMs = 0U;
56:     s_phaseStartMs = 0U;
57:     s_stableMs = 0U;
58: }
59: 
60: void H26_Task3_Start(uint32_t startMs)
61: {
62:     H26_Task3_Reset();
63:     H26_BallControl_Start();
64:     s_startMs = startMs;
65:     s_state = H26_T3_READY;
66: }
67: 
68: void H26_Task3_ForceFault(void)
69: {
70:     H26_T3_EnterFault((s_fault == H26_T3_FAULT_NONE) ?
71:         H26_T3_FAULT_ILLEGAL_STATE : s_fault);
72: }
73: 
74: static void H26_T3_RunPid(uint32_t nowMs,
75:                            float targetCm,
76:                            float toleranceCm,
77:                            float kd,
78:                            uint16_t stableMsTarget,
79:                            H26_Task3State_t nextState)
80: {
81:     H26_BallControlSample_t sample;
82: 
83:     sample = H26_BallControl_Task10msWithPidFeedForward(nowMs,
84:         targetCm,
85:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_KP_MM_PER_CM,
86:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_KI_MM_PER_CM_S,
87:         kd,
88:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_INTEGRAL_LIMIT_CM_S,
89:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_TILT_LIMIT_MM,
90:         0.0f,
91:         H26_T3_TILT_DEADBAND_CM);
92: 
93:     if (sample == H26_BALL_SAMPLE_NEW &&
94:         H26_T3_AbsFloat(H26_BallControl_GetErrorCm()) <= toleranceCm)
95:     {
96:         s_stableMs = H26_T3_AddMs(s_stableMs,
97:             H26_BallControl_GetStableSampleMs());
98:         if (s_stableMs >= stableMsTarget)
99:         {
100:             s_state = nextState;
101:         }
102:     }
103:     else if (sample == H26_BALL_SAMPLE_NEW)
104:     {
105:         s_stableMs = 0U;
106:     }
107: }

112:         targetCm,
113:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_KP_MM_PER_CM,
114:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_KI_MM_PER_CM_S,

117:         H26_T3_FINAL_PID_TILT_LIMIT_MM,
118:         0.0f,

120: }
121: 
122: static void H26_T3_StartMove(H26_Task3State_t moveState,
123:                               RodStepperDirection_t dir,
124:                               uint32_t pulses,
125:                               uint32_t freqHz)
126: {
127:     if (RodStepper_MovePulses(dir, pulses, freqHz) != 0U)
128:     {
129:         s_state = moveState;
130:     }
131:     else
132:     {
133:         H26_T3_EnterFault(H26_T3_FAULT_STEPPER_OUTPUT);
134:     }
135: }
136: 
137: H26_Task3Result_t H26_Task3_Task10ms(uint32_t nowMs)
138: {
139:     if (s_state != H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE1 &&
140:         s_state != H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE2 &&
141:         s_state != H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE3)
142:     {
143:         (void)H26_BallControl_Observe10ms(nowMs);
144:     }
145: 
146:     switch (s_state)
147:     {
148:     case H26_T3_READY:
149:         H26_T3_StartMove(H26_T3_EXTEND_10MM,
150:             H26_T3_GetExtendDirection(),
151:             H26_T3_EXTEND_10MM_PULSES,
152:             H26_T3_EXTEND_10MM_STEP_HZ);
153:         break;
154: 
155:     case H26_T3_EXTEND_10MM:
156:         if (RodStepper_TakeCompletionEvent() != 0U)
157:         {
158:             RodStepper_Stop();
159:             s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
160:             s_state = H26_T3_HOLD_TO_STAGE1;
161:         }
162:         break;
163: 
164:     case H26_T3_HOLD_TO_STAGE1:
165:         if ((nowMs - s_phaseStartMs) >= H26_T3_HOLD_TO_STAGE1_MS)
166:         {
167:             s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
168:             s_stableMs = 0U;
169:             s_state = H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE1;
170:         }
171:         break;
172: 
173:     case H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE1:
174:         H26_T3_RunPid(nowMs,
175:             H26_T3_PID_STAGE1_TARGET_CM,
176:             H26_T3_PID_STAGE1_TOLERANCE_CM,
177:             H26_T3_FINAL_PID_KD_MM_PER_CMPS,
178:             H26_T3_PID_STAGE1_STABLE_MS,
179:             H26_T3_HOLD_TO_RETRACT);
180:         if (s_state == H26_T3_HOLD_TO_RETRACT)
181:         {
182:             s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
183:         }
184:         break;
185: 
186:     case H26_T3_HOLD_TO_RETRACT:
187:         if ((nowMs - s_phaseStartMs) >= H26_T3_HOLD_TO_RETRACT_MS)
188:         {
189:             H26_T3_StartMove(H26_T3_RETRACT_10MM,
190:                 H26_T3_GetRetractDirection(),
191:                 H26_T3_RETRACT_10MM_PULSES,
192:                 H26_T3_RETRACT_10MM_STEP_HZ);
193:         }
194:         break;
195: 
196:     case H26_T3_RETRACT_10MM:
197:         if (RodStepper_TakeCompletionEvent() != 0U)
198:         {
199:             RodStepper_Stop();
200:             s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
201:             s_state = H26_T3_HOLD_TO_STAGE2;
202:         }
203:         break;
204: 
205:     case H26_T3_HOLD_TO_STAGE2:
206:         if ((nowMs - s_phaseStartMs) >= H26_T3_HOLD_TO_STAGE2_MS)
207:         {
208:             s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
209:             s_stableMs = 0U;
210:             s_state = H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE2;
211:         }
212:         break;
213: 
214:     case H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE2:
215:         H26_T3_RunPid(nowMs,
216:             H26_T3_PID_STAGE2_TARGET_CM,
217:             H26_T3_PID_STAGE2_TOLERANCE_CM,
218:             H26_T3_FINAL_PID_KD_MM_PER_CMPS,
219:             H26_T3_PID_STAGE2_STABLE_MS,
220:             H26_T3_DONE);
221:         if (s_state == H26_T3_DONE)
222:         {
223:             s_finalElapsedMs = nowMs - s_startMs;
224:             H26_BallControl_Stop();
225:             s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
226:             s_state = H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE3;
227:         }
228:         break;
229: 
230:     case H26_T3_DONE:
231:         s_finalElapsedMs = nowMs - s_startMs;
232:         H26_BallControl_Stop();
233:         s_phaseStartMs = nowMs;
234:         s_state = H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE3;
235:         break;
236: 
237:     case H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE3:
238:         H26_T3_RunPidForever(nowMs, H26_T3_PID_STAGE2_TARGET_CM);
239:         return H26_T3_RESULT_RUNNING;
240: 
241:     case H26_T3_FAULT:
242:         H26_BallControl_Stop();
243:         return H26_T3_RESULT_FAULT;
244: 
245:     case H26_T3_IDLE:
246:     default:
247:         H26_T3_EnterFault(H26_T3_FAULT_ILLEGAL_STATE);
248:         return H26_T3_RESULT_FAULT;
249:     }
250: 
251:     return (s_state == H26_T3_FAULT) ? H26_T3_RESULT_FAULT :
252:         H26_T3_RESULT_RUNNING;
253: }
254: 
255: H26_Task3State_t H26_Task3_GetState(void) { return s_state; }
256: 
257: uint32_t H26_Task3_GetElapsedMs(uint32_t nowMs)
258: {
259:     if (s_state == H26_T3_IDLE)
260:     {
261:         return 0U;
262:     }
263:     if (s_state == H26_T3_DONE || s_state == H26_T3_FINAL_PID_STAGE3)
264:     {
265:         return s_finalElapsedMs;
266:     }
267:     return nowMs - s_startMs;
268: }
269: 
270: uint32_t H26_Task3_GetFinalElapsedMs(void) { return s_finalElapsedMs; }
271: H26_Task3Fault_t H26_Task3_GetFault(void) { return s_fault; }

59: static int16_t VisionTrack_CentiDegToDeciDeg(int16_t value)
60: {
61:     if (value >= 0)
62:         return (int16_t)((value + 5) / 10);
63:     return (int16_t)((value - 5) / 10);
64: }
65: 
66: static float VisionTrack_AbsFloat(float value)
67: {
68:     return (value >= 0.0f) ? value : -value;
69: }
70: 
71: static float VisionTrack_LimitFloat(float value, float minValue, float maxValue)
72: {
73:     if (value < minValue) return minValue;
74:     if (value > maxValue) return maxValue;
75:     return value;
76: }
77: 
78: static float VisionTrack_MaxFloat(float a, float b)
79: {
80:     return (a >= b) ? a : b;
81: }
82: 
83: static float VisionTrack_CalcTurnLimit(float forwardSpeed)
84: {
85:     float innerSpeed;
86: 
87:     forwardSpeed = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(forwardSpeed,
88:                                           0.0f,
89:                                           VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS);
90:     innerSpeed = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
91:         VISION_TRACK_MIN_INNER_WHEEL_SPEED_CMPS,
92:         0.0f,
93:         forwardSpeed);
94: 
95:     return forwardSpeed - innerSpeed;
96: }
97: 
98: static float VisionTrack_MoveToward(float current,
99:                                      float target,
100:                                      float upStep,
101:                                      float downStep)
102: {
103:     if (target > current)
104:     {
105:         if ((target - current) > upStep)
106:         {
107:             return current + upStep;
108:         }
109:     }
110:     else if (target < current)
111:     {
112:         if ((current - target) > downStep)
113:         {
114:             return current - downStep;
115:         }
116:     }
117: 
118:     return target;
119: }
120: 
121: static int8_t VisionTrack_GetTurnDirection(float value)
122: {
123:     if (value > VISION_TRACK_TURN_REVERSAL_DEADBAND_CMPS)
124:     {
125:         return 1;
126:     }
127:     if (value < -VISION_TRACK_TURN_REVERSAL_DEADBAND_CMPS)
128:     {
129:         return -1;
130:     }
131:     return 0;
132: }
133: 
134: static void VisionTrack_UpdateFilteredErrors(int16_t eyControl,
135:                                              int16_t eaControl,
136:                                              uint8_t degraded)
137: {
138:     float eyNew = (float)eyControl;
139:     float eaNew = (float)eaControl;
140:     float eyAlpha;
141:     float eaAlpha;
142: 
143:     if (!s_hasFilteredErrors)
144:     {
145:         s_filteredEyControl = eyNew;
146:         s_filteredEaControl = degraded ? 0.0f : eaNew;
147:         s_hasFilteredErrors = 1U;
148:         return;
149:     }
150: 
151:     if (degraded)
152:     {
153:         /* A one-boundary ey estimate can jump; ea=0 means unavailable, not straight. */
154:         if (VisionTrack_AbsFloat(eyNew - s_filteredEyControl) <=
155:             VISION_TRACK_EY_JUMP_REJECT_DECI_MM)
156:         {
157:             s_filteredEyControl += VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_EY_FILTER_ALPHA *
158:                 (eyNew - s_filteredEyControl);
159:         }
160:         s_filteredEaControl *= VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_EA_DECAY;
161:     }
162:     else
163:     {
164:         if (s_curveHoldFrames > 0U)
165:         {
166:             eyAlpha = VISION_TRACK_CURVE_EY_FILTER_ALPHA;
167:             eaAlpha = VISION_TRACK_CURVE_EA_FILTER_ALPHA;
168:         }
169:         else
170:         {
171:             eyAlpha = VISION_TRACK_EY_FILTER_ALPHA;
172:             eaAlpha = VISION_TRACK_EA_FILTER_ALPHA;
173:         }
174: 
175:         s_filteredEyControl += eyAlpha *
176:             (eyNew - s_filteredEyControl);
177:         s_filteredEaControl += eaAlpha *
178:             (eaNew - s_filteredEaControl);
179:     }
180: }
181: 
182: static void VisionTrack_UpdateBodyError(const VisionTrackFrame_t *frame,
183:                                         uint8_t degraded)
184: {
185:     float previewHeading;
186:     float previewCompTarget;
187:     float previewDistance;
188: 
189:     /*
190:      * Heading is unavailable in a one-boundary/degraded frame.  Keep the
191:      * previous geometric compensation instead of snapping the virtual point
192:      * back to the camera centre for one frame.
193:      */
194:     previewCompTarget = s_previewCompDeciMm;
195: 
196:     if ((!degraded) &&
197:         (frame->confidence >= VISION_TRACK_TRUSTED_CONFIDENCE))
198:     {
199:         previewHeading = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
200:             s_filteredEaControl,
201:             -VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_EA_LIMIT_DEG /
202:                 VISION_TRACK_EA_DECI_DEG_TO_DEG,
203:             VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_EA_LIMIT_DEG /
204:                 VISION_TRACK_EA_DECI_DEG_TO_DEG);
205:         previewDistance = VISION_TRACK_CAMERA_LOOKAHEAD_MM -
206:             VISION_TRACK_VIRTUAL_LOOKAHEAD_MM;
207:         previewCompTarget = VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_HEADING_SIGN *
208:             previewDistance * previewHeading *
209:             VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_RAD_PER_DECI_DEG /
210:             VISION_TRACK_EY_DECI_MM_TO_MM;
211:         previewCompTarget = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
212:             previewCompTarget,
213:             -VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_MAX_COMP_MM /
214:                 VISION_TRACK_EY_DECI_MM_TO_MM,
215:             VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_MAX_COMP_MM /
216:                 VISION_TRACK_EY_DECI_MM_TO_MM);
217:     }
218: 
219:     /* Do not let a one-frame heading change move the virtual reference. */
220:     s_previewCompDeciMm = VisionTrack_MoveToward(
221:         s_previewCompDeciMm,
222:         previewCompTarget,
223:         VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_STEP_DECI_MM,
224:         VISION_TRACK_PREVIEW_STEP_DECI_MM);
225:     s_bodyEyControl = s_filteredEyControl - s_previewCompDeciMm;
226: }
227: 
228: static float VisionTrack_CalcLargeErrorTurnScale(float bodyEyControl)
229: {
230:     float ratio;
231:     float smoothRatio;
232:     float absEy = VisionTrack_AbsFloat(bodyEyControl);
233: 
234:     if (absEy <= VISION_TRACK_TURN_ATTENUATE_START_DECI_MM)
235:     {
236:         return 1.0f;
237:     }
238: 
239:     if (absEy >= VISION_TRACK_TURN_ATTENUATE_FULL_DECI_MM)
240:     {
241:         return VISION_TRACK_LARGE_ERROR_MIN_TURN_SCALE;
242:     }
243: 
244:     ratio = (absEy - VISION_TRACK_TURN_ATTENUATE_START_DECI_MM) /
245:         (VISION_TRACK_TURN_ATTENUATE_FULL_DECI_MM -
246:          VISION_TRACK_TURN_ATTENUATE_START_DECI_MM);
247:     /* Smoothstep avoids a gain corner when the vehicle enters a deep bend. */
248:     smoothRatio = ratio * ratio * (3.0f - 2.0f * ratio);
249: 
250:     return 1.0f - (1.0f - VISION_TRACK_LARGE_ERROR_MIN_TURN_SCALE) *
251:         smoothRatio;
252: }
253: 
254: static float VisionTrack_CalcTurnCandidate(float dEy, uint8_t confidence)
255: {
256:     float maxOpposingEa;
257:     float headingControl;
258:     float eyRatio;
259:     float largeErrorTurnScale;
260:     float turnCandidate;
261: 
262:     s_turnEy = VISION_TRACK_TURN_SIGN * VISION_TRACK_KY *
263:         s_bodyEyControl;
264: 
265:     headingControl = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
266:         s_filteredEaControl,
267:         -VISION_TRACK_HEADING_CONTROL_LIMIT_DEG /
268:             VISION_TRACK_EA_DECI_DEG_TO_DEG,
269:         VISION_TRACK_HEADING_CONTROL_LIMIT_DEG /
270:             VISION_TRACK_EA_DECI_DEG_TO_DEG);
271:     eyRatio = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
272:         VisionTrack_AbsFloat(s_bodyEyControl) /
273:             VISION_TRACK_HEADING_FULL_EY_DECI_MM,
274:         0.0f,
275:         1.0f);
276:     s_headingScale = VISION_TRACK_HEADING_MIN_SCALE +
277:         (1.0f - VISION_TRACK_HEADING_MIN_SCALE) * eyRatio;
278:     if (confidence < VISION_TRACK_TRUSTED_CONFIDENCE)
279:     {
280:         s_headingScale *= VISION_TRACK_HEADING_LOW_CONF_SCALE;
281:     }
282: 
283:     s_turnEa = VISION_TRACK_HEADING_SIGN * VISION_TRACK_KA *
284:         headingControl * s_headingScale;
285:     s_turnEa = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(s_turnEa,
286:                                       -VISION_TRACK_HEADING_TERM_LIMIT_CMPS,
287:                                       VISION_TRACK_HEADING_TERM_LIMIT_CMPS);
288:     s_turnD = VISION_TRACK_TURN_SIGN * VISION_TRACK_KD * dEy;
289:     s_turnD = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(s_turnD,
290:                                      -VISION_TRACK_D_TERM_LIMIT_CMPS,
291:                                      VISION_TRACK_D_TERM_LIMIT_CMPS);
292: 
293:     /* A large cross-track error must not be reversed by an opposing ea term. */
294:     if ((VisionTrack_AbsFloat(s_bodyEyControl) >=
295:          VISION_TRACK_EY_HEADING_GUARD_DECI_MM) &&
296:         ((s_turnEy * s_turnEa) < 0.0f))
297:     {
298:         maxOpposingEa = VisionTrack_AbsFloat(s_turnEy) *
299:             VISION_TRACK_OPPOSING_EA_RATIO;
300:         s_turnEa = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(s_turnEa,
301:                                            -maxOpposingEa,
302:                                            maxOpposingEa);
303:     }
304: 
305:     turnCandidate = s_turnEy + s_turnEa + s_turnD;
306:     largeErrorTurnScale = VisionTrack_CalcLargeErrorTurnScale(
307:         s_bodyEyControl);
308:     turnCandidate *= largeErrorTurnScale;
309:     return VisionTrack_LimitFloat(turnCandidate,
310:                                   -VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS,
311:                                   VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS);
312: }
313: 
314: static void VisionTrack_UpdateCurveHold(const VisionTrackFrame_t *frame,
315:                                         float severity,
316:                                         uint8_t degraded)
317: {
318:     float absEyMm = VisionTrack_AbsFloat(s_bodyEyControl) *
319:                     VISION_TRACK_EY_DECI_MM_TO_MM;
320:     float absEaDeg = VisionTrack_AbsFloat(s_filteredEaControl) *
321:                      VISION_TRACK_EA_DECI_DEG_TO_DEG;
322: 
323:     if (degraded ||
324:         (severity >= VISION_TRACK_CURVE_TRIGGER_SEVERITY) ||
325:         (absEyMm >= VISION_TRACK_EY_FULL_SLOW_MM) ||
326:         (absEaDeg >= VISION_TRACK_EA_FULL_SLOW_DEG))
327:     {
328:         s_curveHoldFrames = VISION_TRACK_CURVE_HOLD_FRAMES;
329:     }
330:     else if ((!degraded) &&
331:              (frame->confidence >= VISION_TRACK_TRUSTED_CONFIDENCE) &&
332:              (severity <= VISION_TRACK_CURVE_RELEASE_SEVERITY) &&
333:              (s_curveHoldFrames > 0U))
334:     {
335:         s_curveHoldFrames--;
336:     }
337:     else if (s_curveHoldFrames > 0U)
338:     {
339:         /* Release only after consecutive trusted, low-severity full frames. */
340:         s_curveHoldFrames = VISION_TRACK_CURVE_HOLD_FRAMES;
341:     }
342: }
343: 
344: static void VisionTrack_UpdateTurnCommand(float requestedTurn,
345:                                           uint8_t degraded,
346:                                           uint8_t isNewFrame,
347:                                           float forwardLimit)
348: {
349:     int8_t currentDirection;
350:     int8_t requestedDirection;
351: 
352:     if (isNewFrame)
353:     {
354:         requestedTurn = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
355:             requestedTurn,
356:             -VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS,
357:             VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS);
358:         forwardLimit = VisionTrack_CalcTurnLimit(forwardLimit);
359:         requestedTurn = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(requestedTurn,
360:                                                 -forwardLimit,
361:                                                 forwardLimit);
362: 
363:         if (degraded)
364:         {
365:             /* Limit a one-boundary estimate once per K230 frame, not per 10 ms. */
366:             requestedTurn = VisionTrack_MoveToward(
367:                 s_turnTarget,
368:                 requestedTurn,
369:                 VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_TURN_STEP_CMPS,
370:                 VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_TURN_STEP_CMPS);
371:         }
372: 
373:         currentDirection = VisionTrack_GetTurnDirection(s_turnCmd);
374:         requestedDirection = VisionTrack_GetTurnDirection(requestedTurn);
375: 
376:         if ((currentDirection != 0) &&
377:             (requestedDirection != 0) &&
378:             (currentDirection != requestedDirection))
379:         {
380:             if (requestedDirection == s_pendingTurnDirection)
381:             {
382:                 if (s_turnReversalFrames < 0xFFU)
383:                 {
384:                     s_turnReversalFrames++;
385:                 }
386:             }
387:             else
388:             {
389:                 s_pendingTurnDirection = requestedDirection;
390:                 s_turnReversalFrames = 1U;
391:             }
392: 
393:             if (s_turnReversalFrames <
394:                 VISION_TRACK_TURN_REVERSAL_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
395:             {
396:                 /* Decay to zero before a newly observed opposite command is trusted. */
397:                 s_turnTarget = 0.0f;
398:             }
399:             else
400:             {
401:                 s_turnTarget = requestedTurn;
402:                 s_turnReversalFrames = 0U;
403:                 s_pendingTurnDirection = 0;
404:                 s_turnDirectionChangeCount++;
405:             }
406:         }
407:         else
408:         {
409:             s_turnTarget = requestedTurn;
410:             s_turnReversalFrames = 0U;
411:             s_pendingTurnDirection = 0;
412:         }
413:     }
414: 
415:     s_turnCmd = VisionTrack_MoveToward(s_turnCmd,
416:                                         s_turnTarget,
417:                                         VISION_TRACK_TURN_STEP_CMPS,
418:                                         VISION_TRACK_TURN_STEP_CMPS);
419: }
420: 
421: static float VisionTrack_CalcSeverity(float eyControl,
422:                                        float eaControl,
423:                                        float turnCmd)
424: {
425:     float absEyMm;
426:     float absEaDeg;
427:     float eySeverity;
428:     float eaSeverity;
429:     float turnSeverity;
430: 
431:     absEyMm = VisionTrack_AbsFloat(eyControl) *
432:                VISION_TRACK_EY_DECI_MM_TO_MM;
433:     absEaDeg = VisionTrack_AbsFloat(eaControl) *
434:                VISION_TRACK_EA_DECI_DEG_TO_DEG;
435: 
436:     eySeverity = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
437:         absEyMm / VISION_TRACK_EY_FULL_SLOW_MM, 0.0f, 1.0f);
438:     eaSeverity = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
439:         absEaDeg / VISION_TRACK_EA_FULL_SLOW_DEG, 0.0f, 1.0f);
440:     turnSeverity = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
441:         VisionTrack_AbsFloat(turnCmd) / VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS,
442:         0.0f,
443:         1.0f);
444: 
445:     return VisionTrack_MaxFloat(
446:         eySeverity, VisionTrack_MaxFloat(eaSeverity, turnSeverity));
447: }
448: 
449: static float VisionTrack_CalcForwardTarget(float severity,
450:                                            uint8_t degraded,
451:                                            uint8_t curveHoldFrames,
452:                                            uint8_t degradedFrameStreak)
453: {
454:     float target;
455: 
456:     if (degraded)
457:     {
458:         /*
459:          * A single trusted one-boundary frame is common on a bend.  Avoid an
460:          * immediate high-speed-to-crawl transition, but return to the safe
461:          * degraded speed when that condition persists.
462:          */
463:         if (degradedFrameStreak <= VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_GRACE_FRAMES)
464:         {
465:             return VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
466:                 VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_GRACE_SPEED_CMPS,
467:                 VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_SPEED_CMPS,
468:                 VISION_TRACK_MAX_SPEED_CMPS);
469:         }
470: 
471:         return VisionTrack_LimitFloat(VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_SPEED_CMPS,
472:                                       0.0f,
473:                                       VISION_TRACK_MAX_SPEED_CMPS);
474:     }
475: 
476:     target = VISION_TRACK_MAX_SPEED_CMPS -
477:         severity *
478:         (VISION_TRACK_MAX_SPEED_CMPS - VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS);
479: 
480:     target = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(target,
481:                                     VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS,
482:                                     VISION_TRACK_MAX_SPEED_CMPS);
483: 
484:     if (curveHoldFrames > 0U)
485:     {
486:         target = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(target,
487:                                         VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS,
488:                                         VISION_TRACK_CURVE_HOLD_SPEED_CMPS);
489:     }
490: 
491:     return target;
492: }
493: 
494: static void VisionTrack_ResetControlHistory(void)
495: {
496:     s_lastEy = 0;
497:     s_hasLastEy = 0U;
498:     s_lastDEy = 0.0f;
499:     s_severity = 0.0f;
500:     s_forwardTarget = 0.0f;
501:     s_forwardCmdFiltered = 0.0f;
502:     s_turnTarget = 0.0f;
503:     s_turnCmd = 0.0f;
504:     s_leftTarget = 0.0f;
505:     s_rightTarget = 0.0f;
506: 
507:     s_filteredEyControl = 0.0f;
508:     s_filteredEaControl = 0.0f;
509:     s_previewCompDeciMm = 0.0f;
510:     s_bodyEyControl = 0.0f;
511:     s_headingScale = 0.0f;
512:     s_hasFilteredErrors = 0U;
513:     s_turnEy = 0.0f;
514:     s_turnEa = 0.0f;
515:     s_turnD = 0.0f;
516:     s_curveHoldFrames = 0U;
517:     s_degradedFrameStreak = 0U;
518:     s_turnReversalFrames = 0U;
519:     s_pendingTurnDirection = 0;
520:     s_turnDirectionChangeCount = 0U;
521: }
522: 
523: static void VisionTrack_PrepareRun(void)
524: {
525:     VisionTrack_ResetControlHistory();
526:     s_forwardTarget = VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS;
527:     s_forwardCmdFiltered = VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS;
528:     s_leftTarget = VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS;
529:     s_rightTarget = VISION_TRACK_MIN_SPEED_CMPS;
530: }
531: 
532: static uint8_t VisionTrack_IsTransportOk(const VisionTrackFrame_t *frame)
533: {
534:     if (!frame->transportValid) return 0U;
535:     if (App_VisionLink_GetFrameAgeMs() > VISION_TRACK_FRESH_LIMIT_MS)
536:         return 0U;
537:     return 1U;
538: }
539: 
540: static uint8_t VisionTrack_IsFrameUsable(const VisionTrackFrame_t *frame)
541: {
542:     if (!VisionTrack_IsTransportOk(frame)) return 0U;
543:     if (!frame->visionValid) return 0U;
544:     if (frame->mode != VISION_MODE_TRACK) return 0U;
545:     if (frame->confidence < VISION_TRACK_MIN_CONFIDENCE) return 0U;
546:     return 1U;
547: }
548: 
549: static uint8_t VisionTrack_IsDegradedFrame(const VisionTrackFrame_t *frame)
550: {
551:     if (frame->degraded)
552:     {
553:         return 1U;
554:     }
555: 
556:     return ((!frame->leftBoundaryValid) ||
557:             (!frame->rightBoundaryValid)) ? 1U : 0U;
558: }
559: 
560: static void VisionTrack_SafeStop(void)
561: {
562:     App_Control_SetLowSpeedTrackSafety(0U);
563:     App_Control_ForcePWMZero();
564:     Motor_StopAll();
565:     g_carEnable = 0U;
566: }
567: 
568: static void VisionTrack_EnterState(VisionTrackState_t next)
569: {
570:     if (s_state == next) return;
571: 
572:     s_state = next;
573: 
574:     if (next == VISION_TRACK_RUN)
575:     {
576:         VisionTrack_PrepareRun();
577:     }
578:     else
579:     {
580:         VisionTrack_ResetControlHistory();
581:     }
582: }
583: 
584: static void VisionTrack_ApplyMotorCommand(void)
585: {
586:     /* Degraded frames are intentionally permitted to use the lower safe speed. */
587:     s_forwardCmdFiltered = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
588:         s_forwardCmdFiltered,
589:         VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_SPEED_CMPS,
590:         VISION_TRACK_MAX_SPEED_CMPS);
591: 
592:     s_turnCmd = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
593:         s_turnCmd,
594:         -VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS,
595:         VISION_TRACK_TURN_LIMIT_CMPS);
596: 
597:     /*
598:      * Normal RUN retains a rolling inside wheel.  This is stricter than the
599:      * old no-reverse-only limit and prevents a broad curve from degrading
600:      * into an abrupt one-wheel pivot.
601:      */
602:     s_turnCmd = VisionTrack_LimitFloat(
603:         s_turnCmd,
604:         -VisionTrack_CalcTurnLimit(s_forwardCmdFiltered),
605:         VisionTrack_CalcTurnLimit(s_forwardCmdFiltered));
606: 
607:     s_leftTarget = s_forwardCmdFiltered - s_turnCmd;
608:     s_rightTarget = s_forwardCmdFiltered + s_turnCmd;
609: 
610:     g_targetForwardSpeed = s_forwardCmdFiltered;
611:     g_targetTurnSpeed = s_turnCmd;
612:     g_carEnable = 1U;
613:     App_Control_SetLowSpeedTrackSafety(1U);
614:     App_Control_ApplyMotorOutput();
615: }
616: 
617: static void VisionTrack_ClearCurrentFrame(void)
618: {
619:     s_curFrame.sequence = 0U;
620:     s_curFrame.mode = VISION_MODE_IDLE;
621:     s_curFrame.statusFlags = 0U;
622:     s_curFrame.lateralErrorDeciMm = 0;
623:     s_curFrame.headingErrorCentiDeg = 0;
624:     s_curFrame.confidence = 0U;
625:     s_curFrame.transportValid = 0U;
626:     s_curFrame.visionValid = 0U;
627:     s_curFrame.degraded = 0U;
628:     s_curFrame.leftBoundaryValid = 0U;
629:     s_curFrame.rightBoundaryValid = 0U;
630: }
631: 
632: void App_VisionTrack_Init(void)
633: {
634:     s_state = VISION_TRACK_IDLE;
635:     s_acquireCount = 0U;
636:     s_lastAcquireSeq = 0xFFFFU;
637:     s_lostRecoverCount = 0U;
638:     s_lastRecoverSeq = 0xFFFFU;
639:     s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
640:     s_lastOverflowCount = 0U;
641: 
642:     VisionTrack_ClearCurrentFrame();
643:     VisionTrack_ResetControlHistory();
644: 
645: #if VISION_TRACK_DEBUG_ENABLE
646:     s_debugTxLength = 0U;
647:     s_debugElapsedMs = 0U;
648: #endif
649: 
650:     VisionTrack_SafeStop();
651: }
652: 
653: #if VISION_TRACK_DEBUG_ENABLE
654: static void VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar(char value)
655: {
656:     if (s_debugTxLength < (VISION_TRACK_DEBUG_BUFFER_SIZE - 1U))
657:     {
658:         s_debugTxBuffer[s_debugTxLength++] = value;
659:     }
660: }
661: 
662: static void VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(const char *text)
663: {
664:     while (*text != '\0')
665:     {
666:         VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar(*text);
667:         text++;
668:     }
669: }
670: 
671: static void VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32(uint32_t value)
672: {
673:     char digits[10];
674:     uint8_t count = 0U;
675: 
676:     do
677:     {
678:         digits[count++] = (char)('0' + (value % 10U));
679:         value /= 10U;
680:     } while ((value > 0U) && (count < sizeof(digits)));
681: 
682:     while (count > 0U)
683:     {
684:         VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar(digits[--count]);
685:     }
686: }
687: 
688: static void VisionTrack_DebugAppendI32(int32_t value)
689: {
690:     uint32_t magnitude;
691: 
692:     if (value < 0)
693:     {
694:         VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar('-');
695:         magnitude = (uint32_t)(-(value + 1)) + 1U;
696:     }
697:     else
698:     {
699:         magnitude = (uint32_t)value;
700:     }
701: 
702:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32(magnitude);
703: }
704: 
705: static void VisionTrack_DebugAppendFloat2(float value)
706: {
707:     int32_t scaled;
708:     uint32_t magnitude;
709:     uint32_t fraction;
710: 
711:     if (value >= 0.0f)
712:     {
713:         scaled = (int32_t)(value * 100.0f + 0.5f);
714:     }
715:     else
716:     {
717:         scaled = (int32_t)(value * 100.0f - 0.5f);
718:     }
719: 
720:     if (scaled < 0)
721:     {
722:         VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar('-');
723:         magnitude = (uint32_t)(-(scaled + 1)) + 1U;
724:     }
725:     else
726:     {
727:         magnitude = (uint32_t)scaled;
728:     }
729: 
730:     fraction = magnitude % 100U;
731:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32(magnitude / 100U);
732:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar('.');
733:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar((char)('0' + (fraction / 10U)));
734:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendChar((char)('0' + (fraction % 10U)));
735: }
736: 
737: static void VisionTrack_DebugBuildRecord(void)
738: {
739:     s_debugTxLength = 0U;
740: 
741:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString("[vision-track,state=");
742:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32((uint32_t)s_state);
743:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",sequence=");
744:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32((uint32_t)s_curFrame.sequence);
745:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",frameAge=");
746:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32(App_VisionLink_GetFrameAgeMs());
747:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",visionValid=");
748:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32((uint32_t)s_curFrame.visionValid);
749:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",degraded=");
750:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32((uint32_t)s_curFrame.degraded);
751:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",confidence=");
752:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendU32((uint32_t)s_curFrame.confidence);
753:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",ey=");
754:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendI32((int32_t)s_curFrame.lateralErrorDeciMm);
755:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendString(",ea=");
756:     VisionTrack_DebugAppendI32((int32_t)s_curFrame.headingErrorCentiDeg);

813: void App_VisionTrack_Task10ms(void)
814: {
815:     uint8_t isNewSeq;
816:     uint8_t transportOk;
817:     uint8_t frameUsable;
818:     uint8_t controlDegraded;
819:     int16_t eyControl;
820:     int16_t eaControl;
821:     float turnCandidate;
822:     float dEy;
823:     uint32_t curOverflow;
824: 
825:     (void)App_VisionLink_GetLatest(&s_curFrame);
826:     isNewSeq = App_VisionLink_HasNewFrame();
827:     transportOk = VisionTrack_IsTransportOk(&s_curFrame);
828:     frameUsable = VisionTrack_IsFrameUsable(&s_curFrame);
829:     controlDegraded = VisionTrack_IsDegradedFrame(&s_curFrame);
830:     curOverflow = App_VisionLink_GetRxOverflowCount();
831: 
832:     switch (s_state)
833:     {
834:     case VISION_TRACK_IDLE:
835:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
836:         break;
837: 
838:     case VISION_TRACK_ACQUIRE:
839:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
840:         if (!frameUsable)
841:         {
842:             s_acquireCount = 0U;
843:             s_lastAcquireSeq = 0xFFFFU;
844:         }
845:         else if (isNewSeq && s_curFrame.sequence != s_lastAcquireSeq)
846:         {
847:             s_lastAcquireSeq = s_curFrame.sequence;
848:             s_acquireCount++;
849:             if (s_acquireCount >= VISION_TRACK_ACQUIRE_FRAMES)
850:             {
851:                 s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
852:                 s_lastOverflowCount = curOverflow;
853:                 VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_RUN);
854:             }
855:         }
856:         break;
857: 
858:     case VISION_TRACK_RUN:
859:         if (curOverflow != s_lastOverflowCount)
860:         {
861:             VisionTrack_SafeStop();
862:             s_lostRecoverCount = 0U;
863:             s_lastRecoverSeq = 0xFFFFU;
864:             s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
865:             App_VisionLink_Reset();
866:             VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_LOST);
867:             break;
868:         }
869: 
870:         if (!transportOk)
871:         {
872:             VisionTrack_SafeStop();
873:             s_lostRecoverCount = 0U;
874:             s_lastRecoverSeq = 0xFFFFU;
875:             s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
876:             VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_LOST);
877:             break;
878:         }
879: 
880:         if (isNewSeq)
881:         {
882:             if (frameUsable)
883:             {
884:                 s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
885:             }
886:             else if (s_invalidFrameCount < 0xFFU)
887:             {
888:                 s_invalidFrameCount++;
889:             }
890:         }
891: 
892:         if (s_invalidFrameCount >= VISION_TRACK_INVALID_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
893:         {
894:             VisionTrack_SafeStop();
895:             s_lostRecoverCount = 0U;
896:             s_lastRecoverSeq = 0xFFFFU;
897:             VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_LOST);
898:             break;
899:         }
900: 
901:         if (!frameUsable)
902:         {
903:             /*
904:              * Confirm short invalid bursts without using sentinel errors.
905:              * Slow down, keep the curve-speed lock and gently remove steering
906:              * until a usable frame
907:              * returns or the invalid-frame threshold enters LOST.
908:              */
909:             s_lastDEy = 0.0f;
910:             s_severity = 0.0f;
911:             s_curveHoldFrames = VISION_TRACK_CURVE_HOLD_FRAMES;
912:             s_forwardTarget = VISION_TRACK_DEGRADED_SPEED_CMPS;
913:             s_forwardCmdFiltered = VisionTrack_MoveToward(
914:                 s_forwardCmdFiltered,
915:                 s_forwardTarget,
916:                 VISION_TRACK_ACCEL_STEP_CMPS,
917:                 VISION_TRACK_DECEL_STEP_CMPS);
918:             s_turnTarget = 0.0f;
919:             s_turnCmd = VisionTrack_MoveToward(
920:                 s_turnCmd,
921:                 0.0f,
922:                 VISION_TRACK_TURN_DECAY_STEP_CMPS,
923:                 VISION_TRACK_TURN_DECAY_STEP_CMPS);
924:             VisionTrack_ApplyMotorCommand();
925:             break;
926:         }
927: 
928:         eyControl = s_curFrame.lateralErrorDeciMm;
929:         eaControl = VisionTrack_CentiDegToDeciDeg(
930:             s_curFrame.headingErrorCentiDeg);
931: 
932:         if (isNewSeq && !controlDegraded)
933:         {
934:             if (s_hasLastEy)
935:             {
936:                 dEy = (float)(eyControl - s_lastEy);
937:             }
938:             else
939:             {
940:                 dEy = 0.0f;
941:             }
942:             s_lastEy = eyControl;
943:             s_hasLastEy = 1U;
944:         }
945:         else
946:         {
947:             dEy = 0.0f;
948:         }
949:         s_lastDEy = dEy;
950: 
951:         if (isNewSeq)
952:         {
953:             VisionTrack_UpdateFilteredErrors(eyControl,
954:                                              eaControl,
955:                                              controlDegraded);
956:             VisionTrack_UpdateBodyError(&s_curFrame, controlDegraded);
957:             turnCandidate = VisionTrack_CalcTurnCandidate(
958:                 dEy, s_curFrame.confidence);
959:             s_severity = VisionTrack_CalcSeverity(s_bodyEyControl,
960:                                                    s_filteredEaControl,
961:                                                    turnCandidate);
962:             VisionTrack_UpdateCurveHold(&s_curFrame,
963:                                         s_severity,
964:                                         controlDegraded);
965:             if (controlDegraded)
966:             {
967:                 if (s_degradedFrameStreak < 0xFFU)
968:                 {
969:                     s_degradedFrameStreak++;
970:                 }
971:             }
972:             else
973:             {
974:                 s_degradedFrameStreak = 0U;
975:             }
976: 
977:             s_forwardTarget = VisionTrack_CalcForwardTarget(
978:                 s_severity,
979:                 controlDegraded,
980:                 s_curveHoldFrames,
981:                 s_degradedFrameStreak);
982:         }
983: 
984:         s_forwardCmdFiltered = VisionTrack_MoveToward(
985:             s_forwardCmdFiltered,
986:             s_forwardTarget,
987:             VISION_TRACK_ACCEL_STEP_CMPS,
988:             VISION_TRACK_DECEL_STEP_CMPS);
989: 
990:         if (isNewSeq)
991:         {
992:             VisionTrack_UpdateTurnCommand(turnCandidate,
993:                                            controlDegraded,
994:                                            1U,
995:                                            s_forwardCmdFiltered);
996:         }
997:         else
998:         {
999:             VisionTrack_UpdateTurnCommand(s_turnTarget,
1000:                                            0U,
1001:                                            0U,
1002:                                            s_forwardCmdFiltered);
1003:         }
1004: 
1005:         VisionTrack_ApplyMotorCommand();
1006:         break;
1007: 
1008:     case VISION_TRACK_LOST:
1009:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
1010:         if (frameUsable &&
1011:             isNewSeq &&
1012:             s_curFrame.sequence != s_lastRecoverSeq)
1013:         {
1014:             s_lastRecoverSeq = s_curFrame.sequence;
1015:             s_lostRecoverCount++;
1016:             if (s_lostRecoverCount >= VISION_TRACK_ACQUIRE_FRAMES)
1017:             {
1018:                 s_lostRecoverCount = 0U;
1019:                 s_lastRecoverSeq = 0xFFFFU;
1020:                 s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
1021:                 s_lastOverflowCount = curOverflow;
1022:                 VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_RUN);
1023:             }
1024:         }
1025:         else if (!frameUsable)
1026:         {
1027:             s_lostRecoverCount = 0U;
1028:         }
1029:         break;
1030: 
1031:     case VISION_TRACK_STOP:
1032:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
1033:         break;
1034: 
1035:     default:
1036:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
1037:         VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_IDLE);
1038:         break;
1039:     }
1040: }

1057: void App_VisionTrack_HandleKey(uint8_t key)
1058: {

1060: 
1061:     switch (key)
1062:     {
1063:     case 2U:
1064:         if (s_state == VISION_TRACK_IDLE)
1065:         {
1066:             App_VisionLink_Reset();
1067:             App_VisionTrack_Init();
1068:             s_acquireCount = 0U;

1070:             App_VisionLink_SendTrackMode();

1072:         }
1073:         break;
1074: 
1075:     case 3U:
1076:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
1077:         App_VisionLink_SendIdleMode();
1078:         s_invalidFrameCount = 0U;
1079:         VisionTrack_EnterState(VISION_TRACK_STOP);
1080:         break;
1081: 
1082:     case 4U:
1083:         VisionTrack_SafeStop();
1084:         App_VisionLink_SendIdleMode();
1085:         App_VisionLink_Reset();
1086:         App_VisionTrack_Init();
1087:         break;
1088:

1090:         break;
